\n182. /**\n183.  * Validate idToken\n184.  * https://openid.net/specs/openid-connect-core-1_0.html#IDTokenValidation\n185.  * @param idToken - id token from token endpoint\n186.  * @param issuer - {\n194.             throw new Error(\"No ID token\");\n195.         }\n196.         const claims = decodeIdToken(idToken);\n197. \n198.         // The Issuer Identifier for the OpenID Provider MUST exactly match the value of the iss (issuer) Claim.\n199.         if (claims.iss !== issuer) {\n200.             throw new Error(\"Invalid issuer\");\n201.         }\n202.         /**\n203.          * Claim MAY contain an array with more than one element.\n205.          * The ID Token MUST be rejected if the ID Token does not list the Client as a valid audience, or if it contains additional audiences not trusted by the Client.\n206.          * EW: Don't accept tokens with other untrusted audiences\n207.          * */\n208.         if (claims.aud !== clientId) {\n209.             throw new Error(\"Invalid audience\");\n210.         }\n211. \n212.         /**\n213.          * If a nonce value was sent in the Authentication Request, a nonce Claim MUST be present and its value checked\n214.          * to verify that it is the same value as the one that was sent in the Authentication Request.\n215.          */\n216.         if (claims.nonce !== nonce) {\n217. property: ${key}`);\n         return false;\n     }\n     return true;\n };\n const requiredArrayValue = (wellKnown: Record<string, unknown>, key: string, value: any): boolean => {\n     const array = (!idToken) {\n             throw new Error(\"No ID token\");\n         }\n         const claims = decodeIdToken(idToken);\n \n         // The Issuer Identifier for the OpenID Provider MUST exactly match the value of the iss (issuer) Claim.\n         if (claims.iss !== issuer) {\n             throw new Error(\"Invalid issuer\");\n         }\n         /**\n          * The Client MUST validate (!authentication.hasOwnProperty(\"account\") || typeof authentication.account === \"string\")\n51.     ) {\n52.         return {\n53.             issuer: authentication.issuer,\n54.             account: authentication.account,\n55.         };\n56.     }\n57. \n58.     throw new Error(OidcError.Misconfigured);\n59. };\n60. \n61. const isRecord = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> =>\n62.     !!value && typeof value === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(value);\n63. const requiredStringProperty = (wellKnown: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): boolean => {\n64.     if (!wellKnown[key] || !optionalStringProperty(wellKnown, key)) {\n65.         logger.error(`Missing or invalid property: ${key}`);\n66.         return false;\n67.     }\n68.     return true;\n69. };\n70. const optionalStringProperty = (wellKnown: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): boolean => {\n71.     if (!!wellKnown[key] && typeof wellKnown[key] !== \"string\") {\n72.         logger.error(`Invalid property: ${key}`);\n73.         return false;\n74.     }\n75.     return true;\n76. };\n77. const requiredArrayValue = (wellKnown: Record<string, unknown>, key: string, value: any): boolean => {\n78.     const array = wellKnown[key];\n79.     if (!array || !Array.isArray(array) || !array.includes(value)) {\n80.         logger.error(`Invalid property: ${key}. ${value} is required.`);\n81.         return false;\n82.     }\n83.     return true;\n84. };\n85. \n86. /**\n87.  * Validates issuer `.well-known/openid-configuration`\n88.  * As defined in RFC5785 https://openid.net/specs/openid-connect-discovery-1_0.html\n89.  * validates that OP is compatible with Element's OIDC flow\n90.  * @param wellKnown -
